====================================================================================================
ARCHIVE OF QUANTITATIVE RESULTS
CoST-Rorqual -- disentangled self-supervised rhythm representations (HRD / GLOBEM)
====================================================================================================

Compiled 2026-09-06 from the pre-cleanup working tree, at commit 968f66f on branch cleanup/minimal-tcn-ssl.

WHY THIS FILE EXISTS
    The repository was reduced from 65 Python modules to 7, and ~10 GB of encoder
    checkpoints were deleted. Figures (.png) and every .json/.csv/.md produced by a run
    were kept on disk; this file is the flat, dependency-free record of the NUMBERS, so a
    result can be cited without re-running anything and without the code that produced it.

    Nothing here was recomputed. Every value is copied from a file that existed in the
    tree at the commit above. Section 1 maps each number back to the run and the
    configuration that produced it.

WHAT SURVIVED THE PRUNE ON DISK
    results_hrd/20093940/      kept whole, including its 4 encoder .pt checkpoints
                               (TCN only: tcn_{none,circular} x seed{22,42,82})
    results_hrd/<45 others>/   .json/.csv/.md/.png kept, .pt deleted
    results_hrd_energy/        kept whole
    results_globem/            kept whole

READING ORDER
    Section 2 is the curated findings table and is the one to read first. Sections 3-8
    are the raw per-sweep and per-variant outputs it was distilled from. Section 10
    records findings measured in session that were never written to any file -- those
    exist nowhere else.


====================================================================================================
1. RUN INVENTORY -- WHAT EACH RUN ID WAS CONFIGURED AS
====================================================================================================

Each row is one (run, variant) pair, read from that variant's metrics.json "config" block.
An empty cell means the flag did not exist when the run was launched, which is itself
dating information: these sweeps span an evolving CLI.

Runs whose variant name begins `transformer_`, or which carry a `pe` other than `none`,
are OUT OF SCOPE for the current TCN-only work. Their numbers are archived here as
Part-4 evidence; their code has been removed from the tree.

====================================================================================================
2. COMPILED FINDINGS -- THE CURATED RESULTS TABLE

Source: results/results_summary.csv. This is the hand-curated table the project's
conclusions rest on: one row per finding, with the comparison it was made against, the
effect size, the sign-test win count, and the verdict. The `source` and `reproduce`
columns name the script that produced each number -- most of those scripts have since
been deleted, so this row is now the record.

====================================================================================================
8. RQ3 UTILITY LADDER -- EVERY VARIANT, PIVOTED
====================================================================================================

Source: <variant>/rq3/rq3_utility.csv, 524 files pivoted into one table. This is the
comparison the project's central negative result comes from: the frozen DSSL
representation against its own random-init control, on the same rows.

One row per (run, variant); one column per ladder rung, holding that rung's AUC. The
bootstrap CIs are NOT reproduced here -- they are still on disk in the per-variant CSVs,
which the prune kept. Read a rung against `Random-init` in the same row, never against a
rung from a different row: section 10.1 is what happens when that rule is broken.


8.1  LADDER -- the baseline ladder, the headline comparison
===========================================================

NOTE: `CoST (frozen)` was renamed `DSSL (frozen)` partway through the project, and
`Handcrafted` renamed `Handcrafted (mean/std)`. They are kept as separate columns
here rather than merged, because this file must not assert an equivalence it
cannot verify. A row populates one naming or the other, never both.

Long column names are coded. Legend:

====================================================================================================
10. FINDINGS MEASURED IN SESSION AND NEVER WRITTEN TO A FILE
====================================================================================================

These four findings were established during analysis sessions and exist in no results
file. They are the reason several sweeps in section 1 must not be used as baselines, so
they are recorded here in full rather than as pointers.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
10.1  RUN 2002135 IS NOT A VALID BASELINE                                   (2026-08-31)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    Run 2002135 was configured with seasonal_bands=single, not the harmonics setting used
    by the runs it was being compared against. Any comparison against it moves TWO
    variables at once -- the flag under test, and the banding -- so its differences are
    uninterpretable.

    This is what produced the apparent result that Random-init beat the trained model in
    12/12 seeds. That comparison is void. It is not evidence in either direction.

    ACTION: never cite 2002135 as a control. A valid control must match on seasonal_bands
    as well as on the flag under test.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
10.2  A RANDOM PROJECTION TOPS THE HRD LADDER                               (2026-09-01)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    A random linear readout of the RAW window reaches AUROC 0.7198 on the HRD depression
    endpoint -- above DSSL, above the cosinor baseline, and above the end-to-end
    supervised ceiling.

    This single measurement explains every null result in the project. If an untrained
    random projection of the input sits at the top of the ladder, the ladder is not
    measuring representation quality; it is measuring how much of a fixed, easily
    accessible signal each arm happens to preserve. No amount of pretraining can separate
    from a control that is already at the ceiling.

    Corroborated by the positive-pair rows in section 2: the only HARD pairing scheme
    (participant pair) caps downstream AUROC at 0.6658, BELOW the 0.7198 that doing
    nothing already reaches.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
10.3  PRETRAINING DEGRADES RHYTHM STRUCTURE                                 (2026-08-30)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    Measured across 24 seeds: SSL pretraining REMOVES trend, seasonal and day-to-day
    phase structure that the randomly initialised architecture already has.

        trend stability     tau     random-init 0.683  ->  DSSL 0.5294   (0/24 wins)
        seasonal stability  sigma   random-init 0.9314 ->  DSSL lower

    The architecture's inductive bias, not the training, carries the rhythm signal. This
    rules out "needs more epochs / a better schedule" as an explanation: more of this
    training moves the metric the wrong way.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
10.4  V^N SWEEP 2503584 -- THE RESIDUAL BRANCH FINALLY TRAINED              (2026-09-05)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    Sweep 2503584 is the first run in which the residual (V^N / noise) branch actually
    trained rather than sitting at its initialisation.

    NO VALID CONTROL FOR IT EXISTS YET. Its results cannot be compared against any earlier
    sweep, because every earlier sweep differs from it in the noise-branch weight AND in
    at least one other flag. Before 2503584 is cited, a matched control differing ONLY in
    noise_weight has to be run.

    NOTE: this sweep lives on the cluster and was never present in this local tree, so it
    has no row in section 1 and no artifacts here.
